27                                                                                                                27
                           O.5. Controlled approximate-null and false-null tests
28                                                                                                                28

29     The extension varies three departures from the exact action-null example: the                              29

30   ReLU direction, the deterministic action exposure, and a known action-dependent                              30

31   innovation-mean coefficient. Each departure ranges over 0, 1/4096, 1/256, and 1/16;                          31

32   the amplitudes are 1, 16, and 4096. Two original incumbents yield 384 controlled                             32

1    cases, each evaluated on all 322 closed cells. A verified positive allowance replaces                      1

15    The catalogue changes known ReLU directions, action exposures and action-dependent innovation             15

16       means. Harm means a strictly positive lower true-cost difference for an action selected by the         16
     deliberately false-null rule. Corrected decisions pass the nonpositive true-upper check. Cell–cases are
17                                                                                                              17
                 deterministic repeated diagnostics, not independent economic observations.
18                                                                                                              18

19                                                                                                              19
       The corrected gate and the deliberately false-null gate are both replayed. Every
20                                                                                                              20
     selected corrected action is feasible and has a nonpositive true-advantage upper
21                                                                                                              21
     endpoint. For the false-null policy, a strictly positive true-advantage lower endpoint
22                                                                                                              22
     identifies a harmful decision, not merely an uncertain one. The full catalogue retains
23                                                                                                              23
     accepted, blocked and unchanged actions, including cases where large non-nullity
24                                                                                                              24
     makes the verified gate conservative. These are deterministic diagnostics for known
25                                                                                                              25
     perturbed primitives. They are not additional cost-inference samples, an automatic
26                                                                                                              26
     discovery of learned null structure, or a guarantee under an unknown misspecified
27                                                                                                              27
     transition law.

                     APPENDIX P: E CONOMIC SCOPE AND CONCLUSION
30                                                                                                              30

31     The economic contribution is an explicit connection between a policy’s construc- 31
32   tion resources and its implementable cost. A primitive error budget can determine                          32
     128

1    whether to refine the state cover, action search or integration. A complete finite     1

2    tolerance frontier states when one generator returns a certified policy earlier. In- 2
3    dependent comparisons of the returned policies then determine what can be said         3

4    about controller replacement and purchased information. These are different steps;     4

5    none can be replaced by the favorable outcome of another.                              5

6      The constructive backend and exact compiler make positive deterministic state- 6
7    ments under explicit moduli and effective-computability conditions. Their guaran- 7
8    tees are not success probabilities for unrelated nonconvex training algorithms. The    8

9    matched experiments and direct-cost intervals make finite, source-bound claims         9

10   about the declared economies, resource ladders and initial laws. Common-accuracy       10

11   dimension cells do not establish an asymptotic scaling law, and endogenous pre- 11
12   cision within a finite sensor catalogue is not an optimum over all information         12

13   technologies. The original broad theory, applications and adverse experimental         13

14   record remain part of the complete NBO development.                                    14

15     The cost-directed result adds a further positive statement: a feasible acquired      15

16   policy can be improved without sacrificing its incumbent cost guarantee, provided      16

17   its centered advantage is verified. In the executed final-stage specialization every   17

18   studied witness incumbent has a strictly positive identified reduction under the       18

19   declared initial law. Most witness–FVI differences remain unfavorable after both       19

20   methods are repaired. The improvement theorem and this comparative observation         20

21   are consistent: safe improvement relative to an incumbent is not dominance over        21

22   another method.                                                                        22

23     Neural Bellman Operators therefore provide a way to organize continuation            23

24   construction, feasible implementation, certification and economic resource choice      24

25   around the policy actually returned. Whether a particular representation or gen- 25
26   erator is preferable is decided by its full certified frontier and actual economic     26

27   consequences, rather than by the name of its function class.                           27

28                                                                                          28

29                                                                                          29
                  APPENDIX Q: S UBMISSION AND PRESERVATION MAP
30                                                                                          30

31     This article and its technical supplement are the active R54 exposition of the       31

32   same paper. Every R49 main-article and supplementary label is retained in its          32
                                       NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS                                           129

1    corresponding current document. The complete development editions retain the                                 1

2    R48 main and supplementary bodies and add the current theory and evidence, with                              2

3    a reading notice distinguishing historical wording from the active empirical account. 3
4    The original R49 sources remain unchanged under preserved/R49; R48 sources                                   4

5    and earlier theory/application companions are likewise retained. Preservation is                             5

6    not a new mathematical validation of every historical application.                                           6

7      The local revision contains ordinary sources, both immutable R49 evidence                                  7

8    archives, the R48 publication input, newly executed records, generated tables, a                             8

9    point-by-point response and compiled documents. The direct numerical correction                              9

10   retains its original observations and replays the same streams with explicitly out- 10
11   ward cross-policy subtraction. The corrected intervals, not their raw predecessors,                          11

12   govern the active direct-cost tables. Source and evidence identities are checked                             12

13   before a clean offline Git-archive rebuild. The local delivery record distinguishes a                        13

14   completed build from a remote publication: no remote branch or commit is claimed                             14

15   without a confirmed push. This limitation is administrative, not a substitute for the                        15

16   substantive revised manuscript.                                                                              16
